# HabitMinder • Habit Tracker (App Store ID 1253577148) — Full Review Analysis

**App:** HabitMinder • Habit Tracker — *Daily Reminders & Routines*
**Developer (per `manifest.json`):** Kodeon, Inc. · bundle `com.funnmedia.habitminder`
**Reviews analysed:** 1,743 — every single one · 76 storefronts · 16 Nov 2017 → 19 Aug 2026 (106 calendar months)
**Corpus extracted:** 8 Sep 2026 (`manifest.json`) · **written-review mean 3.59** (762×5★, 306×4★, 219×3★, 115×2★, 341×1★)
**Store-level context:** from this repository's ranking snapshot `Tools/habit_apps_ranked.json` — **26,245 public ratings at 4.6**, ranked in 57 storefronts, best search rank 2 (Taiwan), median rank 26, audience "moderate". No live store page was fetched (§1.6).

---

## How to read this

Every claim carries a count, a % of a stated denominator, a signal label and real review IDs, all resolvable in `App Store Reviews/53. HabitMinder • Habit Tracker - Daily Reminders & Routines/reviews.jsonl`. **Part 10 indexes all 1,743 reviews individually**, so any number traces back to exact reviews in one step, and any review traces forward to the themes it carries. **Appendix C lists every review ID behind every theme.**

| Band | Label | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| < 0.1% | Ignore | Not promoted unless safety/legal/data-loss |
| 0.1 – 0.5% | Weak signal | Recorded, cautious wording |
| 0.5 – 1% | Emerging | Worth investigating |
| 1 – 3% | Meaningful | Strong candidate |
| 3 – 5% | Very strong | Should shape roadmap |
| > 5% | High-priority | Strong problem or opportunity |

Theme counts are **non-exclusive** — 3,074 assignments over 1,743 reviews (1.76 per review, median 1). Global percentages always divide by **1,743**; segment and era rates are labelled as such and shown with the global count.

Quotes are given in the original language first; English translations are the analyst's and are marked *EN*.

### ⚠️ Eight warnings before any number here is used

1. **The written corpus is far more negative than the public rating.** Written reviews are 6.64% of public ratings (1,743 of 26,245), and their mean (3.59) sits **1.01★ below** the public average (4.6). In every eligible storefront the gap is 0.75–1.12★ (§7.8). Treat this corpus as the complaint and request channel of a well-rated app, not as a satisfaction survey.
2. **Review volume collapsed, so late-period rates rest on small samples.** 386 reviews in 2018, 115 in 2022, 40 in 2026 (to 19 Aug). The five eras in Part 8 hold 857 / 524 / 207 / 115 / 40 reviews. Era shares for E4 and E5 move by 0.9 and 2.5 percentage points per review.
3. **This is a genuinely multi-market corpus.** 76 storefronts; the US holds 24.56%. 524 reviews (30.06%) are in non-Latin scripts — Chinese 299, Japanese 79, Cyrillic 78, Korean 68 — and were read in the original (Appendix F). Nine storefronts clear 50 reviews (§7.1).
4. **No version field exists.** Every release, price change and incident in Part 8 is dated from what reviewers say and when they say it.
5. **9.75% of the corpus carries no product experience** (`META_LOWINFO` 161 + `META_PREUSE` 9 = 170, mean 4.68). Median body length is 97 characters.
6. **No review gating or burst was found.** Only one review complains of a review prompt (`META_PROMPT`, `4133329823`). The busiest day holds 13 reviews (21 Nov 2017, launch week, when a reviewer says the app sat in the App Store's "new apps we love", `1959810275`). There are 1,182 distinct review days (§1.7).
7. **10 reviews (0.57%, emerging) contradict their own rating** — 8 of them are 5★ complaints. They are kept and listed in full (§5.6, Appendix E).
8. **Ownership claims in reviews are unverified.** Three reviewers link HabitMinder to the owner of the "Done" app or to Reflectly ApS (`9961528922`, `10350224024`, `12966824370`). `manifest.json` names Kodeon, Inc. The report records the claim as reviewer perception only. `is_edited` is true for 90 records (5.16%); 360 records (20.65%) have helpful votes (maximum 274).

---

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

**Executive summary in one line:** HabitMinder is liked for what it was in 2018 — a **clean, reminder-led tracker wired into Apple Health and the Apple Watch, sold for a one-time fee** (`DES_CLEAN` 10.38%, `REM_GOOD` 3.84%, ecosystem praise 4.76%). Since 2023 its reviews are dominated by **how it charges and what happens after people pay**: a "90% off" offer that bills the full price, a countdown pop-up that never ends, earlier lifetime buyers pushed into a new subscription in 2026, statistics views that crash for paying users since late 2024, and a support inbox reviewers describe as silent. **Monetization friction rose from 14.8% of reviews (2017–2019) to 40.1% (2023–Aug 2024) and 45.0% (2026)**; the mean rating fell from 3.90 to 2.55–2.71.

## 0.1 Monetization moved from "pay once" to "pay again", and trust broke with it

**Monetization friction union = 371 reviews (21.29%, high-priority, mean 2.44).** **Billing & trust failures = 133 (7.63%, high-priority, mean 1.81)** — unexpected charges, the full-price "discount", withdrawn entitlements, restore and Family Sharing failures, refund requests and scam accusations (§3.2).

| Era (Part 8) | Reviews | Monetization friction | Billing & trust failures | Mean ★ |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| E1 · Nov 2017–2019 | 857 | 127 (14.8%) | 28 (3.3%) | 3.90 |
| E2 · 2020–2022 | 524 | 99 (18.9%) | 23 (4.4%) | 3.74 |
| **E3 · Jan 2023–Aug 2024** | 207 | **83 (40.1%)** | **44 (21.3%)** | **2.71** |
| E4 · Sep 2024–2025 | 115 | 44 (38.3%) | 26 (22.6%) | 2.55 |
| E5 · 2026 (to 19 Aug) | 40 | 18 (45.0%) | 12 (30.0%) | 2.65 |

The drivers are specific and new in E3:
- **Pop-up upsell that never expires** — `PAY_NAG` = 42 (2.41%, meaningful, mean 1.93), first seen 2021, 39 of 42 from 2023 on. `9742500277` (us, 3★, Mar 2023): *"Every time I've opened the app since the start of 2023 I get shown a “new year, new me” deal on the premium version, with a countdown to when the deal supposedly ends. Except it never ends, it just starts over."* `10463886958` (fr, 1★, Oct 2023): *"it's October and I still have this popup coming EVERY DAY !!"*
- **The "90% off" price that isn't** — `PAY_PROMO` = 35 (2.01%, meaningful, mean 1.83), all Jan 2023 → Feb 2026 (§0.2).
- **Paid users asked to pay again** — `PAY_REGRESS` = 16 (0.92%, emerging, **mean 1.50**), 10 of them Feb–May 2026 (§6.4).
- **Money-grab language** — `PAY_SCAM` = 36 (2.07%, meaningful, **mean 1.08**; 33 of 36 are 1★). It was 0–1.6% of each year's reviews to 2022, then 5.9% (2023), 6.5% (2024) and 8.9% (2025).

## 0.2 The "90% off" offer charges the full price

**`PAY_PROMO` = 35 (2.01%, meaningful, mean 1.83; 1/2/3/4/5 = 21/6/3/3/2; 20 storefronts; 2023-01 → 2026-02).** Reviewers in more than ten currencies describe the same mechanic: a splash screen advertises about 90% off the annual plan, and the App Store sheet shows the undiscounted price. 13 of the 35 also report no reply from support (`SUP_BAD`), and 9 ask for a refund.

| What the offer said → what was charged (reviewer-reported) | Review |
|---|---|
| $7.99 → $79.99 (CA) | `10655270472` (Dec 2023), `11786766831` (Oct 2024) |
| $5.99 → $59.99 / $59.00 (US) | `11314332615` (May 2024), `12488191614` (Mar 2025) |
| $6 → $43.43 (US) | `11297034109` (May 2024) |
| €6.99 → €69.99 (ES, GR, DE) | `10901099777` (Feb 2024), `11865080430` (Oct 2024), `12395519946` (Mar 2025) |
| ¥900 → ¥9,000 (JP) | `13704969988` (Feb 2026) |
| R$20 → R$204 (BR), 299₽ → 2,890₽ (RU), 2,990₸ → 29,990₸ (KZ), 199 TRY → 1,999 TRY (TR) | `10024149607`, `9474648233`, `11424024916`, `11391245873` |

- `12696910935` (us, 1★, May 2025): *"For over a year, a splash screen has been offering a 90% discount, but tapping through only leads to the full-price option with no way to redeem the deal. I've reached out multiple times and have been completely ignored."*
- `13704969988` (jp, 1★, Feb 2026): *"「期間限定:課金9000円を9割引」とあり…900円になっていたので課金したのに、9000円のプランになっていました。"* — *EN:* "it said 'limited time: ¥9,000 plan 90% off', showing ¥900, so I paid — and I was on the ¥9,000 plan."
- `11017180757` (au, 1★, Mar 2024) describes the loop: decline, and the 90% offer returns; accept, and *"it's going to renew at full price!"*

**Reading:** whatever the store-side cause (a wrongly configured introductory offer, an offer for new subscribers shown to returning ones, or intent), reviewers experience it as deception, and it is the most concentrated trust-destroying pattern in the corpus. Several reviewers say it worked for them (`10064369377` *"they may offer $3.99 for one year"*, `13071866426` *"Ich hab pro in 6,99€ bekommen"*), which suggests eligibility rules rather than a universal bug. The corpus cannot tell which.

## 0.3 Statistics — a main reason to pay — crash for paying users

**Statistics broken union = 93 (5.34%, high-priority, mean 2.58)** — `STAT_BUG` 61 (3.50%, very strong, mean 2.70) and `STAT_CRASH` 33 (1.89%, meaningful, mean 2.36).

- **Wrong statistics are persistent (2018–2026):** completed habits shown as incomplete, calendars empty after purchase, percentages that don't add up. `STAT_BUG` peaked at 11.0% of 2021 reviews. `6858470386` (it, 1★, Jan 2021): the developer pointed to *"una funzione nascosta che ricalcola le statistiche ma non risolve il problema"* (*EN:* "a hidden function that recalculates the statistics but doesn't fix the problem"). `7004967188` (fr, 1★, Feb 2021): *"the meditation timer the runs for 11mn and then logs in 22mn46s."*
- **The crash is an event:** 28 of the 33 `STAT_CRASH` reviews fall from Sep 2024 on. They are **21.7% of all E4 reviews (25 of 115)**. Read individually, 32 of the 33 name a statistics, summary, trend, history or calendar view, and 14 name the 7-day (weekly) view specifically. `12609283365` (tw, 1★, May 2025): *"Since the beginning of 2025, accessing the “7-day trend” and “Annual overview of monthly statistics” within the “Abstract” function causes the app to crash."* `13333796053` (gb, 2★, Oct 2025): *"not one [update] resolved the issue on the Summary Tab… Statistics are important to me and if I can't use this part of the service on paid subscription, I will find another app."* `12067473038` (us, 1★, Dec 2024) reset two years of data trying to fix it.
- Paying reviewers over-index on the crash: `STAT_CRASH` lift 2.95 among `PAY_BOUGHT` (11 of 197, §6.1).

## 0.4 Support went from praised to silent

**`SUP_GOOD` = 31 (1.78%, meaningful, mean 4.71) — every one dated Nov 2017 → Mar 2022.** **`SUP_BAD` = 64 (3.67%, very strong, mean 1.58) — 41 of them in 2023–2026**, where they are 11.3% of that period's 362 reviews.

- The earlier reputation was concrete: `5108032721` (us, 5★, Nov 2019) got *"a proactive follow up letting me know the latest update had resolved the issue"*; `8469297159` (ca, 5★, Mar 2022) suggested an icon and *"only a few days later my idea had been implemented"*; `8485670671` (us, 5★, Mar 2022) asked for VoiceOver support and *"soon after a new release came with full voice over compatibility."*
- The later pattern: `10067395807` (gb, 1★, Jun 2023) *"Reached out to the support team over 2 months ago… I think they have abandoned the app"*; `12122960238` (au, 1★, Dec 2024) *"the support email it opens does not send or allow any contact"*; `13689595768` (5★, Jan 2026) *"No update to fix issues on iOS 18 for about a year. No reply from the customers service whatsoever."*
- **Paying reviewers who report silent support average 1.17★** (18 reviews, §6.4).

## 0.5 In China the app hangs on its launch screen

**`BUG_LAUNCH` = 32 (1.84%, meaningful, mean 2.72) — 30 from the `cn` storefront (11.0% of cn's 273 reviews, high-priority at the country denominator), Dec 2022 → Aug 2026.** Reviewers describe the red check-mark splash screen staying up for seconds to many minutes.

- It starts with a named build: `9447929618` (cn, 1★, 29 Dec 2022): *"这个2.8.0版本很差劲，打开页面卡着好久不动，越升级越差"* — *EN:* "version 2.8.0 is terrible, the opening page hangs for ages; every update is worse."
- A diagnostic clue from `9979429377` (cn, 1★, May 2023): *"联网后打不开该软件，必须断网后才行"* — *EN:* "with a network connection it won't open; it only works offline." That points at a blocking network call at launch (for example to an analytics, paywall or ad service that is slow or blocked in mainland China). This is an inference from one review, not a confirmed cause.
- It never went away: `11309719737` (cn, 1★, May 2024) *"每次需要等个 10 多分钟…我还买了会员！"* (*EN:* "every time I wait 10+ minutes… and I bought membership!"); `12966903868` (cn, 1★, Aug 2025) *"多少年了启动画面会卡住很久的 bug 也不修复"* (*EN:* "how many years, and the launch-screen hang still isn't fixed").

## 0.6 The Apple ecosystem is both the differentiator and a failure surface

**Ecosystem praise = 83 (4.76%, very strong, mean 4.51)** — `HK_GOOD` 40, `WATCH_GOOD` 43, `WIDGET_GOOD` 13. **Ecosystem failure = 156 (8.95%, high-priority, mean 3.01)** — `WATCH_BUG` 62, `HK_BUG` 61, `SYNC_BUG` 31, `WIDGET_BUG` 19, `HK_WRITE` 8.

- The integration is what reviewers could not find elsewhere: `2631441908` (gb, 5★, 2018): the Breathe app on the Watch *"automatically marks that as complete"*; `2159236906` (us, 5★, 2018, 17 votes) logs calories from the wrist; `4846107883` (ru, 5★, 2019) praises Watch complications configurable *"практически как сами циферблаты"* (*EN:* "almost like watch faces themselves").
- The failures are the same surfaces: duplicate water entries from the Watch (`2303495789`, `2972959271`), stand and stairs miscounted (`3225288069`, `3494175379`), and timers that **write reading or meditation into Apple Health as exercise** — `HK_WRITE` 8 (0.46%, weak). `4328773622` (nl, 1★, 2019): *"One hour of reading in HabitMinder gave me 60 active minutes."* Weak in count, but it corrupts users' health data, so it is recorded.
- In 2025–2026 the surfaces broke again on new iOS versions: widgets blank on tinted home screens and no longer interactive (`13229780250`, `13635839681`, `13995320819`); the Watch app crashing on launch (`13759712832`, `14182371515`). `WIDGET_BUG` rose to 5.1% (2025) and 7.5% (2026) of each year's reviews.

## 0.7 What works: a simple, reminder-led tracker that changes habits

- **Simplicity:** `DES_CLEAN` = 181 (10.38%, high-priority, mean 4.66), the largest substantive theme. `7933267845` (us, 4★, 2021, 24 votes): *"FINALLY a habit app thats not overpriced or over complicated!"*
- **Design praise union = 261 (14.97%, high-priority, mean 4.64).**
- **Reminders:** `REM_GOOD` = 67 (3.84%, very strong, mean 4.85). `2415120030` (us, 5★, 2018) on the tone of the prompts: *"instead of stating what I'm supposed to do NOW, it asks questions—“Have you walked today?”"*
- **Outcomes:** reported outcome union = 130 (7.46%, high-priority, mean 4.82). `OUT_LIFE` 81 (4.65%, mean 4.91), `OUT_HEALTH` 41, `OUT_MED` 8 — medication (`4017286597` *"I haven't missed a dose since I started using this app"*), stroke (`1959207212`), bariatric care (`2338514034`), alcohol recovery (`5697206540`) and brain injury (`8248119075`).
- **One-time purchase as a reason to buy:** `PAY_ONETIME` = 26 (1.49%, meaningful, mean 4.00), none after Jul 2023. `6845067659` (ca, 5★, 2021): *"Thank you for making it a one time payment and not yearly."*

## 0.8 The scheduling model doesn't fit how people live

**Scheduling-model union = 115 (6.60%, high-priority, mean 3.92)**, plus **reminder problems = 105 (6.02%, high-priority, mean 2.79)**. These come mostly from engaged 4★ users.

- **Frequency:** `CORE_FREQ` 54 (3.10%) — "3 times a week, any day", every other day, a day of the month rather than "beginning/middle/end" (`3638140023`, `2834861951`, `8969578768`).
- **Reminders fire at the wrong time or not at all:** `REM_CONTROL` 56 (3.21%, mean 2.88). 10 reviews say reminders can only be set for 8 am or all arrive then (`9028257319` *"the only reminder that can be set is for 8am"*); 8 hit a notification cap (`3712399255` *"this app has only 20 push notifications so not all your habits will be reminded"*).
- **Pause and archive:** the most-upvoted review in the corpus (274 votes, `6487531368`, jp storefront, 5★, 2020) asks for it: *"in order to keep my page organized I have to delete some habits, which means losing all the records."*
- **Timers that reset:** 13 `CORE_TIMER` reviews describe progress lost when the app is backgrounded, paused or the screen locks, and 9 more say the timer ends without an alert (`3815335235`, `6374998090`, `10556663142`).

## 0.9 The five decisions this corpus supports

1. **Fix or withdraw the "90% off" offer now**, show the charged price on the offer screen, cap how often it appears, and never show it to paying users. Evidence: §0.1–§0.2 — 72 reviews on the offer or its pop-up (4.13%, mean 1.86).
2. **Honour every earlier purchase automatically.** Restore on launch, never gate existing habits behind a new plan, and tell legacy buyers what they keep. Evidence: §6.4 — `PAY_REGRESS` mean 1.50; `13845809502` shows *Restore Purchase* fixed it for one lifetime buyer.
3. **Make statistics work before selling them.** The 7-day trend and year views have crashed for 20 months of reviews. Evidence: §0.3.
4. **Staff a support channel that replies, and publish a status note for known bugs.** Evidence: §0.4.
5. **Remove blocking network calls from app launch** (cn) and **re-certify the Watch app and widgets on every iOS release.** Evidence: §0.5–§0.6.

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role | Records |
|---|---|---|
| `App Store Reviews/53. HabitMinder • Habit Tracker - Daily Reminders & Routines/reviews.jsonl` | Primary corpus — every review read | 1,743 |
| `.../by_country/*.jsonl` | 76 per-storefront files | 1,743 (reconciled, §1.3) |
| `.../manifest.json` | Extraction metadata, per-country counts, rating distribution | — |
| `.../_state.json` | Per-storefront crawl completeness | 144 storefronts polled, all `complete=true` |
| `Tools/habit_apps_ranked.json` | Public rating counts, averages, search ranks and market tiers per storefront (§1.6, Appendix G) | — |

## 1.2 Schema actually present

Every record carries 13 fields: `review_id` · `app_id` · `app_name` · `country` · `country_name` · `rating` · `title` · `body` · `author` · `date` (ISO-8601 UTC) · `vote_count` · `vote_sum` · `is_edited`.

- **Used for findings:** `review_id`, `country`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `date`, `vote_count`, `vote_sum`, `is_edited`.
- **Present but unused:** `author` (pseudonymous; never aggregated), `app_id`, `app_name`, `country_name`.
- **`is_edited` is true for 90 records (5.16%).** Only the flag is available, not the earlier text; 17 reviews say in their text that they were updated (`META_EDIT`).
- **`vote_count` > 0 on 360 records (20.65%).** Maximum 274 (`6487531368`). Votes are cited where notable, never used as weights.
- **Body length:** median 97 characters, mean 165.1.

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Lines in `reviews.jsonl` | 1,743 |
| Unique `review_id` | 1,743 (no duplicate IDs) |
| `manifest.json` `total_reviews` | 1,743 — match |
| Sum of `manifest.json` per-country counts | 1,743 — match |
| Rows across the 76 `by_country/*.jsonl` files | 1,743; union of IDs = 1,743 |
| IDs in country files but not in `reviews.jsonl` / vice versa | 0 / 0 |
| Per-country count mismatches (file vs main vs manifest vs `_state.json`) | 0 |
| `_state.json` complete flag | true for all 144 polled storefronts (76 with reviews) |
| Rating distribution vs manifest | exact match (5:762 · 4:306 · 3:219 · 2:115 · 1:341) |
| Mean rating vs manifest (3.593) | exact match (3.5927) |
| Records classified in `Temp/53-review-classification.py` | **1,743 (100%)** |
| Classified IDs not present in the corpus | 0 |
| Corpus records with no theme assigned | 0 |
| Intra-review duplicate themes | 0 |
| Undefined theme codes used | 0 |
| Theme assignments total | 3,074 (1.76 per review) |
| Declared-but-unused codes | 0 of 85 |

Reproducible with `python3 Temp/53-reconcile.py` and `python3 Temp/53-validate.py`.

## 1.4 Deduplication rule

**No records were removed.** No title+body pair occurs more than once in the corpus. Two near-identical reviews from the kr storefront on 26 Feb 2020 (`5580879385` at 1★ and `5580890253` at 5★) carry the same English text asking for a Family Sharing refund; they have distinct IDs and ratings and are both kept (the 5★ copy is a rating contradiction, §5.6). Every percentage uses **1,743** as the denominator.

## 1.5 Processing method

1. **Parse** all 1,743 records. Sort deterministically by date, then `review_id`, to assign a working index 1–1,743 (`Temp/53-index.tsv`). Reconcile against `by_country/`, `manifest.json` and `_state.json`.
2. **Read every review individually, in the original language**, in chronological order. The corpus was split into 14 files of 125 reviews (`Temp/53-chunks/001–014.txt`) and read end to end: no sampling, no keyword filtering, no skipped chunk. English, Chinese (Simplified and Traditional), Japanese, Korean, Russian, Ukrainian, Kazakh, Turkish, German, French, Spanish, Portuguese, Italian, Dutch, Swedish, Czech, Polish and Arabic reviews were read as written.
3. **Hand-assign themes** from an 85-code, 12-family taxonomy (Appendix B) built inductively while reading. One line per review (`Temp/53-cls/NNN.txt`: `index CODE,CODE`). Assignment is non-exclusive.
4. **Resolve index → review_id** deterministically (`Temp/53-build-classification.py`), asserting that the index table matches `reviews.jsonl`. This emits `Temp/53-review-classification.py`.
5. **Validate programmatically** (`Temp/53-validate.py`): zero unknown IDs, zero unassigned records, zero duplicate keys, zero intra-review duplicate themes, zero undefined codes.
6. **Aggregate** (`Temp/53-aggregate.py`, `Temp/53-extra.py`): global, per-star, per-storefront, market-tier, year, era and month counts; composite unions; paid-segment lifts.
7. **Text sub-cuts are always taken *inside* a hand-coded theme** — for example "8 am" wording within `REM_CONTROL`, or dollar figures within `PAY_*` reviews. They are labelled as text sub-cuts wherever they appear. No finding rests on a keyword search over the whole corpus.
8. **Re-read high-impact clusters before writing:** every `PAY_PROMO`, `PAY_REGRESS`, `PAY_CHARGE`, `STAT_CRASH`, `BUG_LAUNCH`, `SUP_BAD`, `META_CONTRA`, `HK_WRITE`, `OUT_MED`, `OUT_ADHD` and `PRIV` review, and every review quoted in this report (`Temp/53-show.py`).

**No automated classifier was used.** The error risk is judgement error on short or ambiguous reviews. The calls most likely to move a number are disclosed in Appendix D.

## 1.6 Limitations — read these before citing any figure

1. **No live store data.** No App Store page or developer site was fetched. **Every price, tier, trial and feature description is reconstructed from review text** and carries reviewers' own errors. Contradictions are reported, not resolved.
2. **One external source is used:** this repository's `Tools/habit_apps_ranked.json` snapshot (undated in-file). It supplies public rating counts, averages, search ranks and the `market_tier` label (`rich` / `volume`), which §7.2 adopts as its high-spend definition.
3. **Selection bias is large.** Writers are 6.64% of raters and average 1.01★ lower (§7.8).
4. **Small late samples.** 2023–2026 hold 362 reviews in total. The trends in Part 8 are large enough to survive that, but single-month figures are not.
5. **No developer responses are in the data.** Several reviewers quote or react to replies (`4146776919`, `7004967188`, `9028257319`); the replies themselves are not available.
6. **Storefront ≠ language ≠ nationality.** English text appears in the cn storefront (`13689595768`, `13777493893`) and Chinese text in the us storefront (`10845511093`). Country findings describe storefronts.
7. **The app title's "Routines" and the ADHD/medical users are small segments.** `OUT_ADHD` = 3 and `OUT_MED` = 8; they are reported for what they are (§4.8).

## 1.7 Solicited reviews, bursts and review integrity

- **No gating.** Unlike some apps in this repository, HabitMinder shows no review-to-unlock pattern. One reviewer objects to repeated prompts (`META_PROMPT` 1, `4133329823`: *"毎回アプリを開いて記録しようとレビューを書けとしつこく促され非常に不快"*, *EN:* "every time I open the app to log, it nags me to write a review — very unpleasant").
- **No bursts.** Busiest days: 21 Nov 2017 (13 reviews), 23 Feb 2019 (9), 20 Nov 2017, 28 May 2018 and 24 Feb 2019 (7 each). Launch week (16–22 Nov 2017) holds 45 reviews and reads as organic launch traffic (App Store feature, `1959810275`). The 23–24 Feb 2019 cluster (16 reviews) spans eight storefronts (ru 5, us 3, ca 2, tr 2, de, kr, jp, lt), with 12 of 16 at 4–5★ and varied, specific texts; it also reads as organic.
- **Fake-review allegations:** none.
- **Contradictions:** 10 (0.57%) — §5.6 and Appendix E.
- **A same-text pair** from one storefront and date with opposite ratings (`5580879385`/`5580890253`) is disclosed in §1.4.

---

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL (review-derived)

## 2.1 What the product is, per reviewers

A **habit tracker built around reminders and Apple Health**, with pre-built habits (drink water, walk, stand, stretch, read, meditate, brush teeth, take medication) and custom habits measured in counts or minutes.

| Surface | Review-derived description | Theme evidence | Representative IDs |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Habit list ("Today")** | Habits grouped by time of day (all day / morning / afternoon / evening), swipe to complete or skip, dark mode, alternate app icons | `DES_CLEAN` 181 · `DES_CUSTOM` 39 · `CORE_ORDER` 14 | `4356490129` `9580577060` `3399514212` |
| **Goal types** | Count per day (water, squats) or minutes per day; quick-add buttons; no custom units | `CORE_EDIT` 57 · `DES_UNITS` 47 | `2038634081` `3644522063` `4365954820` |
| **Reminders** | Notification prompts phrased as questions, per-habit sounds; default morning time; a system cap on scheduled notifications | `REM_GOOD` 67 · `REM_CONTROL` 56 · `REM_ANNOY` 28 | `2415120030` `3712399255` `9028257319` |
| **Built-in timers** | Countdown timers for reading, stretching, meditation, a breathing exercise | `CORE_TIMER` 52 | `3358770351` `4609758212` `3815335235` |
| **Apple Health** | Reads steps, stairs, stand, mindful minutes, exercise, water; writes timed habits back | `HK_GOOD` 40 · `HK_BUG` 61 · `HK_WANT` 31 · `HK_WRITE` 8 | `2631441908` `2915448839` `4328773622` |
| **Apple Watch** | Watch app, configurable complications, logging from the wrist | `WATCH_GOOD` 43 · `WATCH_BUG` 62 · `WATCH_WANT` 11 | `2159236906` `4846107883` `2303495789` |
| **Widgets** | Today-view and home-screen widgets; interactive check-off in later builds | `WIDGET_GOOD` 13 · `WIDGET_BUG` 19 · `WIDGET_WANT` 18 | `6946319037` `13229780250` `3586986691` |
| **Statistics** | Summary, 7-day trend, calendar, year-by-month, streaks, completion rate; export mentioned in 2022 | `STAT_GOOD` 15 · `STAT_BUG` 61 · `STAT_WANT` 50 · `STAT_CRASH` 33 | `6487531368` `8389450436` `12609283365` |
| **Devices** | iPhone, iPad, Mac app (from ~2020) and iCloud sync | `IPAD` 29 · `MAC` 13 · `SYNC_BUG` 31 · `SYNC_WANT` 10 | `5692057530` `6709188227` `11165885767` |
| **Sibling apps** | WaterMinder and HealthView from the same studio; cross-purchasers are common | `USER_SIBLING` 17 | `5611006227` `3529760821` `6657730615` |
| **Later additions** | Vacation mode and sleep from Health (Jan 2022, `8253504398`); habit notes; Siri completion (`8041655449`) | — | `8253504398` `8041655449` |

## 2.2 What it costs, per reviewers — a nine-year price history

No price list is available outside review text. The ladder below is reconstructed from dated reviews (figures found inside `PAY_*`-coded reviews, §1.5):

| When (review date) | Reported price / model | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Nov 2017 – 2019 | **Free for 3 habits**; unlimited habits as a **one-time purchase** ("five bucks", $4.99, $6.99, $7, $7.99) **or** a subscription; tiers named "unlimited" and "unlimited subscription" confuse buyers | `1930741082` `3444353028` `4016363600` `2036148762` `2127875492` |
| 2018 | 7-day trial on the subscription; description claims **Family Sharing**, which in-app purchases don't support | `3395705944` `2108309583` `3226095725` |
| May 2018 (cn) | **Price rise** ¥25/¥30 → ¥68 lifetime | `2617451296` `2608211653` `3328155414` |
| 2019 – 2020 | Yearly $4.99 → $9.99; one-time $7.99 → $12–13; €13.99 / 14 € lifetime in DE and FR | `3827323643` `5002932242` `5055664516` `6300752427` `4380319331` `4731951054` |
| Jan 2020 (cn) | ¥128 unlimited (*"2018 年无限制才 25 啊"*) | `5469128371` |
| 2021 – 2022 | $19.99 / $20 unlimited; $27 CAD | `8173719445` `8969578768` `9421439545` |
| 2023 → | **Annual subscription is the only plan reviewers see** ($39.99; over €40; ¥148–398 cn) plus the recurring **"90% off" offer** | `9757059002` `9782732023` `10147101911` `9548074755` `9683344106` `10200978029` |
| 2024 | AU$99.99 annual; offers at 10% of that | `11017180757` |
| Feb–May 2026 | Legacy premium users asked for **$14.99–$20/year**; a new "AppLife" plan appears | `13766633834` `13801600222` `14014376727` `14047317847` |

Text census inside `PAY_*` reviews: dollar figures appear in 31 reviews; one-time / lifetime wording in 31 (26 of them before 2023); "90%" in 21 (all 2023–2026); subscription wording in 122. These are text counts, not themes.

## 2.3 Free / paid / trial / unclear classification

| Capability | Status per reviews | Confidence | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Download, up to **3 habits**, reminders, Health reading | **Free** | High | `PAY_CAP3` 89 |
| More than 3 habits | **Paid** (one-time until ~2022, then subscription) | High | `1926677091` `10465535774` |
| Detailed statistics / graphs | **Paid** in several builds | Medium-high | `2865460099` `3848798143` `4987536893` `8302078405` |
| Calendar progress view | **Paid** (2022) | Medium | `8302078405` |
| Adding habits or changing a habit's settings once over the free limit | **Paid** — reviewers over the limit hit the paywall when editing | Medium | `10202235764` `13269079790` |
| iCloud sync across iPhone / iPad / Mac | **Unclear** — used by paying reviewers; frequently fails (`SYNC_BUG` 31) | Medium | `6928470411` `7093425717` `11165885767` |
| Family Sharing | **Not supported** despite listing | High (8 reviews) | `2108309583` `3172407902` `6831293208` |
| Trial | **7-day free trial** on the subscription; several reviewers say they were billed a year | Medium | `3395705944` `4515168100` `6638609482` `12658527707` |
| Previously bought lifetime premium (2026) | **Unclear** — some legacy buyers see it removed; *Restore Purchase* recovered it for one | Medium | `13845809502` `14047317847` |
| Ads | **No third-party ads described**; the "ads" reviewers complain about are the app's own premium offers | Medium | `14089831243` `12301378085` |

**Unclear from reviews:** whether "AppLife" is a cross-app bundle; which regions or cohorts get the 90% price; whether the Chinese launch stall is region-specific or build-specific.

## 2.4 One structural observation about the model

The monetization history is a sequence of **re-scopings of what buyers thought they owned**: a one-time unlock (2017–2021) → annual subscription for new users with a perpetual "limited-time" discount (2023) → earlier buyers asked to subscribe for features they had been using (2026). The re-scoping reviews are the lowest-rated monetization themes in the corpus: `PAY_SCAM` mean 1.08, `PAY_CHARGE` 1.38, `PAY_REGRESS` 1.50, `PAY_PROMO` 1.83. Where earlier purchases were visibly honoured, reviewers wrote some of the warmest reviews (`2075726152`, `6845067659`, `13845809502`).

## 2.5 Positioning versus the reviewing population

The listing subtitle names **Daily Reminders & Routines**, and that matches the corpus: reminders (`REM_*` family 172, 9.87%) and Apple Health / Watch (`HK` 131, Watch-related 113) are the core. What the listing doesn't show is the audience: outcome reviews centre on water, exercise and medication (`OUT_HEALTH` 41, `OUT_MED` 8), and a small set of older, disabled or recovering users rely on it (`1959207212`, `8248119075`, `8485670671`, `6952282273` *"I sure could use some step by step help for this baby boomer!!"*). These are the users most exposed to confusing billing.

## 3.3 Prioritised negative themes

### N1 — Monetization friction — 371 (21.29%), high-priority, mean 2.44
The largest negative cluster, and it grew (§0.1). Components: `PAY_CAP3` 89 (5.11%), `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` 86 (4.93%, mean 2.63), `PAY_WALL` 44 (2.52%, mean 2.18), `PAY_NAG` 42, `PAY_REFUND` 40, `PAY_SCAM` 36, `PAY_PROMO` 35, `PAY_CHARGE` 34, `PAY_CONFUSE` 23, `PAY_REGRESS` 16, `PAY_RESTORE` 14, `PAY_SUB_NEG` 12, `PAY_PRICE_RISE` 9, `PAY_FAMILY` 8. IDs: `9742500277` `10655270472` `13766633834` `10465535774` `4515168100` `5469128371`.

### N2 — Crashes — `BUG_CRASH` = 133 (7.63%), high-priority, mean 2.82
Three waves: launch month (Nov 2017, 10 of 62 reviews), 2018–2020 background crashes on iOS updates (iOS 11.3 `2366136178`, iOS 13 `4823904178` `4832903775`, "after the newest update" `5060149220`), and the 2024–2026 statistics-view crash (27 of 32 crash reviews from Sep 2024 on are `STAT_CRASH`, §0.3). Paid crash reviewers average 2.50 (§6.4). IDs: `1930741082` `4201979505` `6504371746` `8512051848` `12609283365`.

### N3 — Statistics broken — 93 (5.34%), high-priority, mean 2.58
§0.3. IDs: `4344403490` `6858470386` `8069707266` `11662916325` `13333796053`.

### N4 — Support silence — `SUP_BAD` = 64 (3.67%), very strong, mean 1.58
§0.4. Earlier, the complaint was that the in-app contact button needed a mail account (`2155682745`, `2563809309`, `3599764886`); later, that nobody replies. IDs: `5299667616` `9486645084` `10067395807` `11639436686` `12122960238`.

### N5 — Apple ecosystem failure — 156 (8.95%), high-priority, mean 3.01
§0.6. Largest components `WATCH_BUG` 62 (3.56%) and `HK_BUG` 61 (3.50%). IDs: `2303495789` `2479189057` `3832875558` `6800089096` `13759712832`.

### N6 — Reminder problems — 105 (6.02%), high-priority, mean 2.79
`REM_CONTROL` 56 (3.21%) — can't pick the time, notification cap, no snooze, no per-habit off switch; `REM_ANNOY` 28 (1.61%) — a burst of 10–15 notifications at 8 am (`3747841106` *"I had 64 notifications in just a few days"*), reminders for habits already done (`2871731780`, `3235623582`, `9905500578`); `REM_SOUND` 14; `REM_FAIL` 13. For an app named *HabitMinder*, reviewers find this the most ironic failure (`5967476975` *"How come an app meant for one to develop habits and especially having a name “Habitminder” doesn't give out notifications (reminders)?"*).

### N7 — Launch stall in China — `BUG_LAUNCH` = 32 (1.84%), meaningful, mean 2.72
§0.5. IDs: `9447929618` `9492157990` `9979429377` `11309719737` `14373827038`.

### N8 — Timers lose progress — `CORE_TIMER` = 52 (2.98%), meaningful, mean 3.54
Read individually, 13 of the 52 describe a timer losing progress when backgrounded, paused or when the screen sleeps (`4444144780`, `5473458011`, `5873965949`, `6458372004`, `6628706096`), and 9 more say it ends without an alert (`3640606535`, `5623248461`, `5991387602`). `3815335235` (my, 2★, 2019): *"after 90 mins you had a call and after the conference call you come back and found that the counter is no longer running and now you've only read 0 minutes."* `10556663142` (ca, 1★, Nov 2023) left after years because the screen now powers off during a 30-minute meditation.

### N9 — Units and language — `DES_UNITS` 47 (2.70%) · `DES_LOC` 19 (1.09%)
US units for water, weight and distance frustrate non-US users (`2370670399` *"Stuck with Dopey American units"*, `2942843899` *"In Europe we have no idea what a US Fl Oz is!"*, `2544263095` *"跑步不支持公里"*, *EN:* "running doesn't support kilometres", `8743237851` notifications in ounces with litres selected). Localisation quality complaints come from ru, br, it, se, kr and jp (`8144824208` flags a machine-translated Korean label; `5805036362` *"あなたは日本語をもっと勉強が必要です"*, *EN:* "you need to study more Japanese").

### N10 — Privacy — `PRIV` = 7 (0.40%), weak, mean 1.86
Two reviewers read the privacy policy and object to Facebook and Firebase SDKs (`7797619285`: *"Facebook Ads conversion tracking (Facebook pixel)… Google Analytics for Firebase"*; `7881617666` *"sends data to Facebook Graph"*). Others report unexplained App Store password or Apple ID prompts on launch (`3066587541`, `3190215855`, `3632700324`). Weak in count, but recorded because health data is involved.

## 3.4 Prioritised positive themes

### P1 — Simple and clean — `DES_CLEAN` = 181 (10.38%), high-priority, mean 4.66
13.5% of E1 reviews, 5.8–8.2% later. Tr (21.2%), kr (14.9%) and de (14.0%) lead (§7). IDs: `2086555489` `3693795375` `4846107883` `7933267845` `14345426907`.

### P2 — Reported outcomes — 130 (7.46%), high-priority, mean 4.82
§0.7. IDs: `2378643057` `4017286597` `5324340299` `6740702700` `9669522126`.

### P3 — Reminders that work — `REM_GOOD` = 67 (3.84%), very strong, mean 4.85
US-heavy (36 of 67). None after May 2024. IDs: `2415120030` `3181982525` `5820044323` `8485670671` `8777851077`.

### P4 — Apple ecosystem praise — 83 (4.76%), very strong, mean 4.51
§0.6. IDs: `2159236906` `2631441908` `4016363600` `5108032721` `6570320235`.

### P5 — Price is fair / one-time purchase — `PAY_PRICE_OK` 47 (2.70%, mean 4.79) · `PAY_ONETIME` 26 (1.49%, mean 4.00)
Both collapse after 2022: `PAY_PRICE_OK` has 2 reviews from 2023 on, `PAY_ONETIME` has none after Jul 2023. `4116093972` (us, 5★, 2019): *"RAISE YOUR PRICES… I would genuinely pay more to support these creators."*

### P6 — Support and developer responsiveness — `SUP_GOOD` = 31 (1.78%), meaningful, mean 4.71
All before Apr 2022 (§0.4). IDs: `1936741100` `2021139655` `5108032721` `8270833232` `8469297159`.

### P7 — Customisation — `DES_CUSTOM` = 39 (2.24%), meaningful, mean 4.74
Icons, colours, per-habit sounds, dark mode and alternate app icons (`4016363600`, `4356490129` *"man kann sogar das App Icon auf dem Homescreen ändern"*, *EN:* "you can even change the app icon").

## 3.7 What is *not* a finding

- **ADHD** (`OUT_ADHD` 3) and **accessibility** (`DES_A11Y` 2) are below or near the ignore line; §4.8 records them.
- **Review prompts** (`META_PROMPT` 1) — ignore.
- **Price rises** (`PAY_PRICE_RISE` 9, 0.52%) are a cn-storefront 2018–2020 story (7 of 9) and are superseded by the 2023 model change.

---

# PART 4 — WHAT ACTIVE USERS SAY ABOUT THE PRODUCT

This part excludes monetization (Part 6) and reads the feature-level themes from people who clearly use the app.

## 4.1 The check-off loop: fast, but too many taps for some

Reviewers like the swipe-to-complete list and the completion sound (`1975390550` *"it makes a psychologically satisfying sound"*). The friction is taps: `DES_UX_NEG` = 38 (2.18%, mean 2.89). `5577124103` (gb, 3★, 2020): *"It should be a single tap to complete one once the reminder pops up."* `6715323631` (ua, 4★, 2020) compares it unfavourably with Productive's one-swipe check. `9777723429` (my, 2★, 2023): editing habits *"are a nightmare - you have to tap in multiple times."*

## 4.2 Reminders: the reason people install, and a top frustration

| Theme | n | % | Mean ★ |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `REM_GOOD` | 67 | 3.84% | 4.85 |
| `REM_CONTROL` | 56 | 3.21% | 2.88 |
| `REM_ANNOY` | 28 | 1.61% | 2.61 |
| `REM_SOUND` | 14 | 0.80% | 3.14 |
| `REM_FAIL` | 13 | 0.75% | 2.54 |

The core tension: reminders are tied to a time-of-day section rather than a clock time. An evening habit reminds at 8 am (`8218421549` *"When I added the sleep activity, the fixed reminder is set to 8 am. I need a reminder to go to sleep not wake up."*). A 30-reminder habit list hits iOS's scheduled-notification cap (`6329692631`, `8342223672`). Reminders ignore completion (`9905500578`: *"the app reminds me to stand, or to walk, when it should know based on my steps being tracked that I've met the goal"*).

## 4.3 Apple Health and the Watch

- **Reads that go wrong:** stand counted while asleep (`2969091667`, `3225288069`), steps not updating on iPad or Mac (`5513693479`, `6485399432`), sleep stages from iOS 16 only partly read (`9404007222`), doubled Health statistics (`10988144171`).
- **Writes that go wrong:** `HK_WRITE` 8 — reading, meditation or relax timers become exercise minutes or active calories (`2399781275`, `2509894345`, `4941084864`, `7641900979`).
- **Watch:** loved for logging and complications (`WATCH_GOOD` 43, mean 4.70), broken for sync, duplicates and not opening (`WATCH_BUG` 62, mean 2.85). kr (8.0%) and cn (5.1%) report the Watch failing most often by share.

## 4.4 Statistics: what reviewers want to see

Reviewers ask for three things, in order of frequency: a **calendar of what was done** (`2096325228`, `2546162811`, `8447296290`); **totals and trends in units, not just completion %** (`3469612741` *"a total of 1000 push ups"*, `8391806786` *"if your goal is to sleep 8 hours a day and you've been sleeping for 7 hours for the past week, the summary shows 0%"*); and **a view that doesn't punish** weekly or skipped habits (`3594922582`, `7342524208`). What they got in 2024–2026 was a crash (§0.3).

## 4.5 Scheduling: weekly habits, skips and the day boundary

`CORE_FREQ` 54 and `CORE_SKIP` 20 describe the same failure: a daily grid for non-daily behaviour. `3638140023` (us, 4★, 2019): a 3×/week habit marks Monday and Tuesday failed; support suggested Skip, but that *"skips that task for the week."* `3828842561` (ca, 3★, 2019): *"if I set a habit to be 2 times weekly, I have to do the habit twice in one day in order for it to be considered complete."* No `CORE_FREQ` review appears after Mar 2024; the late corpus is about billing and crashes, not because the model changed (no reviewer says it did).

## 4.6 Devices and sync

Sync failures (`SYNC_BUG` 31, 1.78%) are concentrated among payers (lift 3.42, §6.1): the iPhone completes a habit that the iPad doesn't count (`6709188227`), a purchase doesn't carry to another device (`6928470411`), completed tasks revert to incomplete across devices (`11388158456`). The Mac app exists from 2020 (`5692057530`, `5857453619`) but one reviewer couldn't download it in Japan after paying (`9960634122`).

## 4.7 Scope: what people don't want

Few reviewers ask for social features; two explicitly ask for none (`2096325228` *"请不要…加入社交功能"*, *EN:* "please don't add social features"; `2540846249` *"不要社交，不要鸡汤，please just leave it simple."*). `4284181386` (cn, 5★, 21 votes) asks the developer *not* to add a calendar, to keep the app focused. Simplicity is defended as the product.

## 4.8 Small segments recorded for completeness

- **ADHD / ADD** — `OUT_ADHD` = 3 (0.17%, weak): `3118663766` (us, 5★, 2018, ADD for 40 years: *"this is a life saver"*), `9876062679` (au, 5★, 2023, *"I've used the app to make a game out of the tasks!"*), `10607661695` (us, 1★, 2023: *"my a.d.d didn't want to use the app"*).
- **Accessibility** — `DES_A11Y` = 2 (0.11%, weak): `8173719445` (us, 4★, Dec 2021) on unlabeled VoiceOver buttons; `8485670671` (us, 5★, Mar 2022) confirms a fix shipped. Recorded because it is a positive, verifiable developer response.
- **Medical and recovery users** — `OUT_MED` = 8 (0.46%, weak, mean 4.62).

**The 1★ share rose four-fold across eras:** E1 104 of 857 (12.1%) · E2 81 of 524 (15.5%) · **E3 82 of 207 (39.6%)** · E4 55 of 115 (47.8%) · E5 19 of 40 (47.5%).

## 5.1 Five stars (n = 762, 43.72%)

Substantive 5★ praise is **simplicity** (`DES_CLEAN` 18.2% of the band), **outcomes** (`OUT_LIFE` 10.0%), **reminders that work** (`REM_GOOD` 8.0%), **a purchase that felt fair** (`PAY_BOUGHT` 8.7%, `PAY_PRICE_OK` 5.0%) and **the Watch** (`WATCH_GOOD` 4.5%). 17.8% of the band is content-free (`META_LOWINFO`). Differentiator signals: all 30 `META_RECOMMEND` reviews and 26 of the 31 `SUP_GOOD` reviews are 5★. 32 five-star reviews mention a crash, usually one the developer fixed (`2021139655` *"It crashes - now fixed"*, `8270833232`).

## 5.2 Four stars (n = 306, 17.56%)

The **request band**. 24 of the 54 `CORE_FREQ` reviews (44.4%) and 11 of the 21 `CORE_BAD` reviews are 4★, as are 14 of 31 `HK_WANT`. A typical 4★ names the single missing thing: *"If only the habit name could hold more characters"* (`6253481302`), *"Only reason it gets 4 stars… it will consistently send reminders for activities you have already completed"* (`3235623582`), *"so close to perfect… ability to group activities"* (`4267292883`).

## 5.3 Three stars (n = 219, 12.56%)

Paid-but-disappointed and quota-limited: `PAY_BOUGHT` 10.0%, `PAY_CAP3` 9.6%, `STAT_BUG` 8.2%, `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` 8.2%, `WATCH_BUG` 7.3%, `BUG_CRASH` 7.3%, `DES_UNITS` 5.9%. `8885882361` (br, 3★, 2022): *"I have used this app for 4 years and it still has some bugs like wrong counting of habits completed."*

## 5.4 Two stars (n = 115, 6.60%)

`PAY_BOUGHT` 16.5%, `PAY_CAP3` 13.0%, `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` 12.2%, `HK_BUG` 8.7%, `BUG_CRASH` 8.7%, `REM_CONTROL` 7.8%. The 2★ reviewer usually still likes the design and is explaining why they won't pay or will leave (`11639436686`: *"I have used this app for years, and absolutely love how it functions. The reason for the low rating is the customer service."*).

## 5.5 One star (n = 341, 19.56%)

`PAY_BOUGHT` 18.2% (62), `BUG_CRASH` 15.0% (51), `SUP_BAD` 13.8% (47), `PAY_SCAM` 9.7% (33), `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` 8.5%, `PAY_CHARGE` 8.5% (29), `PAY_REFUND` 8.2% (28), `PAY_WALL` 6.5%, `PAY_NAG` 6.5% (22), `PAY_PROMO` 6.2% (21).

**Three churn drivers are visible in 1★ reviews:**
1. **A charge the reviewer didn't expect** — `PAY_CHARGE` (85.3% of it is 1★), `PAY_PROMO` (60.0%), `PAY_SCAM` (91.7%).
2. **A paid feature that doesn't work** — crash, statistics, Watch or sync after paying (§6.4).
3. **Nobody answers** — 47 of 64 `SUP_BAD` reviews (73.4%) are 1★.

## 5.6 Reviews contradicting their own rating — `META_CONTRA` = 10 (0.57%, emerging)

8 are 5★, 1 is 3★ and 1 is 2★. Types:

| Type | Example |
|---|---|
| 5★ with a complaint or refund demand | `1925705563` (*"App doesn't work… I want my money back"*); `12943319713` (br, *"Recebi uma oferta com 90% de desconto, mas foi cobrado 100% do valor"*, *EN:* "I got a 90%-off offer but was charged 100%"); `13689595768` (*"Terrible customer service"*) |
| Same text posted twice at opposite ratings | `5580890253` (5★) duplicating `5580879385` (1★), both asking to cancel a payment |
| Low rating with positive text | `2761355706` (jp, 2★, *"使いやすい"*, *EN:* "easy to use"); `2782269925` (gb, 3★, *"This app has helped me so much, I love it"*) |

The full list is in Appendix E. At 0.57% of the corpus they cannot move a headline figure, and they are kept in every count.

## 5.7 Theme direction is not fixed by rating

- `PAY_CAP3` has 20 five-star reviews ("three is enough for me").
- `BUG_CRASH` has 32 five-star reviews.
- `PAY_BOUGHT` has 62 one-star and 66 five-star reviews.

Star rating alone is therefore not used anywhere in this report as a proxy for feature preference.

# PART 6 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

**Evidence tiers.**
- **Direct:** `PAY_BOUGHT` = 197 (11.30%, high-priority, mean 3.09) — first-person statements of buying, subscribing, being on a trial or holding premium.
- **Indirect interest:** `PAY_ONETIME` without a purchase statement = 16 (valuing the one-time option); `PAY_CONFUSE` = 23 (pre-purchase questions about what a tier includes).
- **No conversion rate is claimed**; written reviewers are not a purchase funnel.

## 6.1 Who the paying reviewers are

**The paying segment turned from satisfied to aggrieved.** Payers in E1–E2 (2017–2022): 143, mean 3.44. Payers in E3–E5 (2023–2026): 54, **mean 2.15**. By 2026 paying reviewers are 11 of 40 reviews (27.5%) and average 1.82 (§8.2).

| Payer sub-segment | n | Mean ★ |
|---|---:|---:|
| Paid **and** reports value (fair price, one-time, clean/pretty, outcome) at 4–5★ | 42 | 4.81 |
| Paid **and** a reliability failure (crash, stats, Health, Watch, widget, sync, reminder) | 82 | 2.46 |
| Paid **and** statistics broken | 24 | 2.25 |
| Paid **and** a billing / trust failure | 44 | 2.14 |
| Paid **and** entitlement withdrawn (`PAY_REGRESS`) | 10 | **1.50** |
| Paid **and** support never answered | 18 | **1.17** |

The first row is *why people pay*. Payers over-index most on **`PAY_RESTORE` (lift 6.32)**, **`PAY_REGRESS` (5.53)**, **`PAY_PRICE_OK` (3.95)**, **`SYNC_BUG` (3.42)**, **`PAY_ONETIME` (3.40)** and **`STAT_CRASH` (2.95)**: they value a fair one-time price, and what breaks for them is the entitlement itself, sync across their devices and the statistics they paid for.

## 6.2 What triggers a purchase (in reviewers' own words)

1. **More than three habits, after a short trial of the free tier.** `4016363600` (us, 5★, 2019): *"I spent about a week using the free version… before I decided to pay for it. I have like 25 habits now!"* `3809738342` (jp, 5★): *"たくさんの事がやりたかったから思い切って有料のを買いました"* (*EN:* "I wanted to do lots of things, so I took the plunge and bought the paid version").
2. **A one-time price.** `2075726152` (de, 5★, 2018, 19 votes): *"da man nicht wie in vielen anderen Apps ein Abo abschließen muss sondern einfach einmalig Geld bezahlt"* (*EN:* "because unlike many apps you don't need a subscription, you just pay once"). `3787746538` (us, 5★): *"(Thank god for no subscription model)"*. `8969578768` (us, 3★, 2022) bought lifetime because it combined Apple Health sync, unlimited tasks and a one-time price.
3. **Apple Health and Watch integration.** `4079685774` (us, 5★, 2019): *"The watch complications and amount of customization they offer already makes it the best habit app."* `5108032721` (us, 5★).
4. **Statistics.** `2258509452` (cn, 4★, 2018): *"希望能添加更详细和强大的统计和报表功能，25元支持你们继续开发！"* (*EN:* "please add more detailed statistics — ¥25 to support your development"). `12734062444` (ru, 1★, 2025) bought premium *"с расширенной статистикой"* (*EN:* "with extended statistics") and it crashes.
5. **Supporting the developer.** `4116093972` (us, 5★) and `8777242811` (in, 5★, 2022: *"upgraded it to support developers!"*).
6. **The discount, when it worked.** `9876062679` (au, 5★, 2023): *"Very happy that I got a special on the subscription!"*; `10096889134` (us, 4★, 2023) took the "new year new me" promo.

| Only three free habits | `PAY_CAP3` | 89 | 5.11% | High-priority | 3.04 | `3078624683` `6247504677` `14293413489` |
| Price too high / not worth it | `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` | 86 | 4.93% | Very strong | 2.63 | `2617147342` `3804351852` `9421439545` `11017180757` |
| Paywall in general / "not really free" | `PAY_WALL` | 44 | 2.52% | Meaningful | 2.18 | `5549483897` `8580817713` `8950673885` |
| Unclear tiers | `PAY_CONFUSE` | 23 | 1.32% | Meaningful | 3.48 | `2036148762` `3759793889` `5523950378` `11574969207` |
| Subscription model itself | `PAY_SUB_NEG` | 12 | 0.69% | Emerging | 2.42 | `2112781844` `9523405070` `13568218349` |
| No Family Sharing | `PAY_FAMILY` | 8 | 0.46% | Weak | 3.75 | `2108309583` `3226095725` `8170857136` |
| Asked to pay before trying | (inside `PAY_WALL` / `PAY_NAG`) | — | — | — | — | `6819482362` *"I was asked to pay too soon, without really trying the app"* · `9720640125` *"Let me try before upselling"* · `13269079790` |
| Distrust after the offer or re-subscription | `PAY_PROMO` / `PAY_REGRESS` | 35 / 16 | — | — | 1.83 / 1.50 | `10350224024` *"I will be regretfully avoiding this developer because their pricing policies"* |

## 6.4 Post-purchase problems (why payers turn 1★)

| Problem | Payers (segment n of 197) | Segment mean ★ | Global n | IDs |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| App crashes after paying | 28 (14.2%) | 2.50 | 133 | `1930741082` `2694748659` `4201979505` `6504371746` |
| Statistics wrong or crashing | 24 (12.2%) | 2.25 | 93 | `11662916325` `12734062444` `12759551874` `13080891181` |
| Support never answered | 18 (9.1%) | 1.17 | 64 | `5299667616` `9486645084` `10067395807` `12122960238` |
| Refund requested | 13 (6.6%) | 2.23 | 40 | `2108309583` `5355987117` `9960634122` `12122960238` |
| Sync across own devices fails | 12 (6.1%) | 2.92 | 31 | `6709188227` `7093425717` `11165885767` |
| Purchase not restored / not on other device | 10 (5.1%) | 2.80 | 14 | `2121752548` `2690013219` `6928470411` `9461241780` |
| **Earlier purchase no longer honoured** | 10 (5.1%) | **1.50** | 16 | `13765322086` `13777493893` `13801600222` `14047317847` `14051267496` |
| Charged unexpectedly | 6 (3.0%) | 1.83 | 34 | `2719010612` `2898302369` `5055664516` |

**The legacy-purchase break, in order.** `9961528922` (gb, May 2023) alleges the same owner degraded lifetime upgrades in the "Done" app. `10566333653` (cn, Nov 2023): *"之前买断的VIP,怎么又变成订阅收费了？二次收费，太不合理"* (*EN:* "the VIP I bought outright has turned into a subscription? Charging twice is unreasonable"). `11904370385` (us, Nov 2024) and `12154855959` (ca, Jan 2025: *"I had previously PAID to have full access to this app. And now they're forcing a yearly subscription."*). Then a cluster of 10 in Feb–May 2026: `13766633834` (a 5-year user asked for $15/year to use the Reading habit), `13801600222` (*"all of a sudden I can't use the additional habits I created… without purchasing a $14.99 upgrade"*), `14047317847` (jp: a lifetime buyer treated as free under a new "AppLife" scheme), `14051267496` (a premium member clicking through an AppLife subscription ad to log a habit).

**The counter-evidence matters:** `13845809502` (us, 4★, Mar 2026, 8 votes) was about to leave a bait-and-switch review, found an in-app purchase from 2019, tapped *Restore Purchase*, and *"the app went back to normal and no more pop ups."* Some of the 2026 cluster is therefore a restore failure rather than a policy — which the app could fix silently by restoring on launch. `13875929821` (tr, 1★) reports premium rights until November deleted with no restore.

## 6.5 Refunds — `PAY_REFUND` = 40 (2.29%, meaningful, mean 1.68)

Triggers, in order of frequency seen while reading:
- **the "90% off" charge** (9: `10901099777`, `11297034109`, `11714336606`, `11996312131`);
- **a trial or an app open that became a year's charge** (`4605017698`, `6638609482` *"1주 트라이얼을 선택했는데... 1년치 결제가 됐어요"*, *EN:* "I chose the 1-week trial and was charged for a year", `9712675638`, `9834204551`, `10577123237`, `10799071715`);
- **Family Sharing that doesn't exist** (`2108309583`, `5580879385`, `5580890253`);
- **a crash or broken feature right after buying** (`3048477717`, `5355987117`, `7062119500`, `8048552552`).

## 6.6 Signals that would change a paying user's mind

From the payer reviews read, in order of how often they recur:
1. a charged price that matches the advertised price;
2. earlier purchases honoured without asking;
3. statistics that open;
4. a reply from support;
5. sync that works across iPhone, iPad, Watch and Mac;
6. flexible frequency and reminder times.

None of these is a lower price. Payers who talk about price mostly call it fair (`PAY_PRICE_OK` lift 3.95).

Nine storefronts clear 50 reviews and are analysed individually (§7.3–§7.7): **us 428 · cn 273 · jp 92 · kr 87 · ru 79 · ca 67 · tr 66 · tw 56 · de 50** — 1,198 reviews (68.73%). The other 67 storefronts hold 545 reviews and are **[limited evidence]**: included in every global figure, with no standalone conclusion drawn. Near-misses: br 44, gb 41, es 39, mx 39.

## 7.2 Market groups — defined, not assumed

**High-spend markets.** Membership comes from the `market_tier = "rich"` label in `Tools/habit_apps_ranked.json` (the repository's own classification). 27 storefronts in this corpus carry it; together they hold **1,299 reviews (74.53%), mean 3.57**. Because the US dominates, the group is also shown **excluding us: 871 reviews, mean 3.61**.

**High-review-volume markets.** No download data is available, so volume is used as a disclosed proxy:
- **(a)** storefronts with ≥50 written reviews — the nine above, 1,198 reviews, mean 3.64;
- **(b)** by public rating count in the snapshot — us 5,866 · cn 3,864 · tw 2,253 · jp 2,163 · kr 1,874 · ca 890 · ru 713 · tr 645 · mx 603 · de 596.

The two lists agree except mx (603 public ratings, 39 written reviews — [limited evidence]).

| Group | Storefronts | Reviews | Mean ★ | Share written 2023+ | Mean ★ 2023+ | Monetization friction | Billing & trust | Reliability |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Global | 76 | 1,743 | 3.59 | 20.8% | 2.65 | 21.3% | 7.6% | 25.2% |
| **High-spend (`rich`)** | 27 | 1,299 | 3.57 | 21.5% | 2.63 | 20.9% | 7.2% | 26.1% |
| High-spend excl. us | 26 | 871 | 3.61 | 20.1% | 2.68 | 21.4% | 6.9% | 27.4% |
| **High-review-volume (≥50)** | 9 | 1,198 | 3.64 | 19.1% | 2.62 | 19.9% | 6.9% | 27.0% |
| Snapshot `volume` tier | 23 | 393 | 3.63 | 17.0% | 2.61 | 22.9% | 8.9% | 22.9% |
| Not ranked in snapshot | 26 | 51 | 3.82 | 31.4% | 3.12 | 17.6% | 7.8% | 21.6% |

**Reading:** the high-spend group mirrors the global picture, because it *is* most of the corpus. The `volume` tier leans more on price: `PAY_PRICE_OK` 4.6% and `PAY_CAP3` 5.9% of its reviews, and 13.2% content-free reviews.

**Language groups (for comparison; composite, not markets):**

| Group | Reviews | Mean ★ | Distinctive themes (segment rate) |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| English-primary (us, ca, gb, au, nz, ie) | 560 | 3.46 | `REM_GOOD` 7.9% · `SUP_BAD` 6.1% · `PAY_CAP3` 5.5% · `REM_CONTROL` 5.4% · 25.5% written 2023+ |
| East Asia (cn, tw, hk, jp, kr, sg) | 526 | 3.74 | reliability 31.9% · `BUG_LAUNCH` 5.9% · `STAT_WANT` 4.9% · `WATCH_BUG` 4.6% |
| Russian-speaking (ru, ua, kz, by, kg, uz, tj) | 121 | 3.55 | reliability 31.4% · `DES_UNITS` 6.6% · `STAT_BUG` 6.6% · `PAY_ONETIME` 5.0% |
| Spanish / Portuguese (es, mx, br, pt and Latin America) | 167 | 3.51 | **monetization friction 34.1%** · billing & trust 12.0% · `PAY_CAP3` 10.8% · `PAY_PROMO` 4.8% |
| Other Europe (de, fr, it, nl, ch, at, Nordics, CEE) | 194 | 3.41 | `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` 9.3% · `PAY_CAP3` 7.2% · `HK_BUG` 6.2% |

- **Explicitly anti-social:** the few requests about community ask for none (`2096325228`, `2540846249`).

- 2023+ reviews (21, mean 2.76) include the ¥900 → ¥9,000 offer (`13704969988`) and the AppLife entitlement loss (`14047317847`).

Triggers: a year billed for a one-week trial (`6638609482`), an automatic renewal (`7348146608`), Family Sharing (`5580879385`, `5580890253`), a crash right after paying ₩19,000 (`5355987117`), and an accidental purchase on first open (`10799071715`).
- **Watch and sync:** `WATCH_BUG` 7 (8.05%), `SYNC_BUG` 4 (4.60%), `IPAD` 4 (4.60%).
- Only 8 reviews (9.2%) date from 2023 on, so kr says little about the late-period monetization change.

The gap is consistent (0.75–1.12★) across very different markets, so it reflects who writes reviews, not one market's mood. The snapshot's public averages are cumulative, and the written corpus shows the 2023–2026 decline (Part 8) that the cumulative average would hide.

## 7.9 Localisation and units

- `DES_UNITS` 47 (2.70%) across 20 storefronts — cn 9, us 5, gb 4, ua 3, tw 3, ru 3, fr 3. Water in US fl oz, weight in lb, running in miles (`2084114971`, `2592196005`, `9438206006` *"体重の単位にkgを加えてもらえると嬉しいです"*, *EN:* "I'd be glad if you added kg for weight").
- `DES_LOC` 19 (1.09%) across 12 storefronts, **none after Feb 2023** — machine-translated or mixed-language UI (`2443295086` lock-screen reminders alternating Chinese and English; `6838520896` *"Dåligt översatt till svenska"*, *EN:* "poorly translated into Swedish"), and requests for French, Italian and Brazilian Portuguese (`1925872873`, `5533693679`, `4018192204`).

## 7.10 What is not supported by country data

- **No country-level price elasticity.** Price figures are reviewer-reported and span nine years.
- **No standalone conclusion for any storefront under 50 reviews**, including br (44), gb (41), es (39) and mx (39).
- **No cause for the cn launch stall** beyond one reviewer's offline observation (§0.5).
- **No conclusion that the late-period problems are regional.** They appear in every group that has late reviews (§7.2 table, "Mean ★ 2023+" 2.61–3.12).

---

# PART 8 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 8.1 Method

- **Calendar years** (2017 from 16 Nov; 2026 to 19 Aug) and **five eras** bounded by events visible in the reviews:
  - E1 ends Dec 2019 (the one-time-purchase period, most review volume);
  - E3 starts Jan 2023, with the first `PAY_PROMO` (`9474648233`) and the cn launch stall (from `9447929618`, 29 Dec 2022);
  - E4 starts Sep 2024, with the first statistics-view crash of the wave (`11771772770`);
  - E5 is 2026, the legacy re-subscription cluster.
- Rates are shares of each period's reviews.
- **E4 (115) and E5 (40) are small.** A trend is claimed only where the direction holds across adjacent periods or the absolute count is concentrated in time.

## 8.2 Volume and rating by year

| Year | Reviews | % of corpus | Mean ★ | 1/2/3/4/5 | 1★ share | Paying reviewers (`PAY_BOUGHT`) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|
| 2017 (from 16 Nov) | 98 | 5.62% | 3.78 | 14/5/18/13/48 | 14.3% | 3 (3.1%) |
| 2018 | 386 | 22.15% | 3.94 | 40/23/49/84/190 | 10.4% | 41 (10.6%) |
| 2019 | 373 | 21.40% | 3.89 | 50/22/39/69/193 | 13.4% | 45 (12.1%) |
| 2020 | 263 | 15.09% | 3.87 | 35/15/39/35/139 | 13.3% | 29 (11.0%) |
| 2021 | 146 | 8.38% | 3.50 | 28/15/16/30/57 | 19.2% | 17 (11.6%) |
| 2022 | 115 | 6.60% | 3.77 | 18/5/15/25/52 | 15.7% | 8 (7.0%) |
| 2023 | 135 | 7.75% | 2.69 | 56/9/19/23/28 | 41.5% | 21 (15.6%) |
| 2024 | 108 | 6.20% | 2.69 | 44/13/10/15/26 | 40.7% | 11 (10.2%) |
| 2025 | 79 | 4.53% | 2.54 | 37/6/10/8/18 | 46.8% | 11 (13.9%) |
| 2026 (to 19 Aug) | 40 | 2.29% | 2.65 | 19/2/4/4/11 | 47.5% | 11 (27.5%) |

| Era | Window | Reviews | Mean ★ | 1/2/3/4/5 |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| E1 | Nov 2017–2019 · launch, 3-habit free tier, one-time purchase | 857 | 3.90 | 104/50/106/166/431 |
| E2 | 2020–2022 · price rises, stats & sync bugs, slower updates | 524 | 3.74 | 81/35/70/90/248 |
| E3 | Jan 2023–Aug 2024 · '90% off' offers, launch stalls, silent support | 207 | 2.71 | 82/19/28/33/45 |
| E4 | Sep 2024–2025 · statistics-view crash era | 115 | 2.55 | 55/9/11/13/27 |
| E5 | 2026 (to 19 Aug) · paid users forced into new subscription | 40 | 2.65 | 19/2/4/4/11 |

Volume fell every year after 2018, from 386 to 40, while the mean held at 3.50–3.94 through 2022, **then dropped to 2.69 in 2023** and has stayed at 2.54–2.69 since.

## 8.5 What the trend data does *not* support

- **Why volume fell.** Fewer users, fewer review prompts and a smaller App Store surface would all produce it; the corpus cannot separate them.
- **Whether the late problems were fixed.** Several late themes end in the data because reviews end, not because a reviewer says they were fixed.
- **Whether the 2026 re-subscription is policy or a restore failure.** Both are evidenced (§6.4).
- **Any causal link** between the pricing changes and the rating decline. They coincide in time; the reliability failures coincide too.

## 8.6 Persistent, improving and disappearing themes

- **Persistent:** `DES_CLEAN` in every year (4.4–15.3%); `HK_BUG` in every year (2.1–5.2%); `PAY_PRICE_HIGH` in every year (3.1–8.9%); content-free reviews (5.6–11.4%).
- **Declining share:** `PAY_CAP3` from 9.2% (2017) and 8.2% (2021) to 2.5% (2025–2026), as reviewers stopped discussing the free tier and started discussing the subscription.
- **No longer reported (absence of reviews, not proof of a fix):** `DES_LOC` after Feb 2023; `CORE_FREQ` after Mar 2024.
- **Disappearing positives:** `SUP_GOOD` 0 after Mar 2022; `PAY_ONETIME` 0 after Jul 2023; `REM_GOOD` 0 after May 2024; `DES_PRETTY` 0 in 2025–2026; `WATCH_GOOD` from 4.3% (2022) to 0% (2026).

---

# PART 9 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Every item cites the finding it rests on. **F** = immediate fix · **M** = monetization change to test · **R** = product and retention work · **Q** = research question the corpus cannot answer.

## 9.1 Immediate fixes

| # | Fix | Why (finding) | Size of evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | **Audit the in-app "90% off" offer.** Confirm offer eligibility (new vs returning subscribers) and store configuration. Show the exact first charge and renewal price on the offer screen. Stop showing an offer the viewer can't redeem. Proactively refund reviewers who were charged full price. | §0.2 | `PAY_PROMO` 35 (2.01%, mean 1.83); 13 with no support reply |
| F2 | **Restore purchases automatically on launch and on every device**, and grandfather every earlier one-time or lifetime buyer into whatever plan replaced it (including "AppLife"). Tell them in-app what they keep. | §6.4 | `PAY_REGRESS` 16 (mean 1.50) · `PAY_RESTORE` 14 · `13845809502` restore fixed it |
| F3 | **Fix the statistics-view crash** (7-day trend, year by month, history, calendar) on iOS 18+. Test with long histories and many habits (`11808267771`: 3 months × 20 habits). | §0.3 | `STAT_CRASH` 33; 28 since Sep 2024; payer lift 2.95 |
| F4 | **Fix the support channel**: an in-app contact that works without a configured Mail account, an auto-acknowledgement, and a public known-issues note. | §0.4 | `SUP_BAD` 64 (mean 1.58); paid ∩ `SUP_BAD` mean 1.17 |
| F5 | **Remove blocking network calls from launch** and test cold start with no network and with slow or blocked endpoints (mainland China). | §0.5 | `BUG_LAUNCH` 32; cn 10.99% |
| F6 | **Re-certify the Watch app and widgets on each iOS release** (tinted and clear home screens, interactive widgets, Watch launch). | §0.6, §8.4 | `WIDGET_BUG` 19 · `WATCH_BUG` 62; 10 in 2025–2026 |
| F7 | **Stop writing non-exercise timers to Apple Health as workouts** (reading, relaxing, breathing), or make the write-back explicit per habit. | §0.6, §4.3 | `HK_WRITE` 8 — weak, included on data-integrity grounds |

## 9.2 Monetization changes to test

| # | Test | Why (finding) | Watch for |
|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | **Cap upsell frequency** (e.g. once a week), never show it to payers, and drop the resetting countdown. | §0.1 | `PAY_NAG` 42 (mean 1.93); `9742500277` `14089831243` |
| M2 | **Offer a lifetime option again** next to the annual plan, priced to protect revenue. | §0.7, §6.1 | `PAY_ONETIME` lift 3.40 and `PAY_PRICE_OK` lift 3.95 among payers |
| M3 | **Test the free tier:** 5 habits, or a full-feature trial that ends without auto-billing. Measure retention, not reviews. | §3.5, §6.3 | `PAY_CAP3` 89; 35 of them are content at 4–5★, so a bigger tier is not self-evidently better |
| M4 | **Make trials explicit:** charge date and amount on the trial screen, plus a reminder 24 hours before conversion. | §6.5 | `PAY_CHARGE` 34 (mean 1.38); `4515168100` `6638609482` `12658527707` |
| M5 | **Remove "Family Sharing" from the listing** unless the purchase actually supports it. | §2.3 | `PAY_FAMILY` 8 |

## 9.3 Product and retention work

| # | Work | Why (finding) |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | **Per-habit reminder clock times**, several per day, snooze from the notification, and **suppression once a habit or its Health goal is met.** | §4.2 — `REM_CONTROL` 56, `REM_ANNOY` 28 |
| R2 | **Flexible frequency:** N times per week on any day, every N days, a day of the month; weekly habits must not fail a day. | §4.5 — `CORE_FREQ` 54 |
| R3 | **Archive and pause** without deleting history. | §0.8 — 274-vote `6487531368`; `CORE_SKIP` 20 |
| R4 | **Timers that survive backgrounding** (store the start time, compute elapsed) and **always alert at the end** via a local notification and Watch haptic. | §3.3 N8 — 22 of 52 `CORE_TIMER` |
| R5 | **Locale-aware units and a translation pass**; allow custom units. | §7.9 — `DES_UNITS` 47, `DES_LOC` 19 |
| R6 | **Statistics in units, not only completion %:** totals, averages, a calendar of done days. | §4.4 — `STAT_WANT` 50 |
| R7 | **Limit goals, multiple sessions per day, and a custom end of day.** | §3.6 — `CORE_BAD` 21, `CORE_MULTI` 12, `CORE_DAYSTART` 9 |
| R8 | **Publish and minimise third-party analytics** in a health-adjacent app. | §3.3 N10 — `PRIV` 7 (weak; trust-sensitive) |

## 9.4 Research questions this corpus cannot answer

- **Q1** Which cohorts see the 90% offer at full price — new users, lapsed subscribers, or particular storefronts?
- **Q2** How many legacy buyers lost access because of a policy change, and how many only because restore failed?
- **Q3** What blocks launch in mainland China — network, SDK, or build?
- **Q4** Did review volume fall because users left or because prompts stopped?
- **Q5** Does the three-habit free tier convert or churn? Written reviews cannot measure conversion.

## 9.5 What to do first

1. **F1 + F2** — they stop active harm to paying users and are the main source of 1★ reviews since 2023.
2. **F3** — the feature people pay for.
3. **F4** — every other failure is worse without replies.
4. **F5, F6** — platform stability in the two largest storefronts.
5. Then **M1** and **R1–R4**, which serve the engaged 4★ users who stayed.

---

# PART 9.1 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## A. Counting rules

1. **Denominator:** all global percentages divide by 1,743; country rates by that storefront's count; era and year rates by that period's count; payer rates by 197.
2. **Non-exclusive themes:** a review carries every theme it supports; family and composite unions count each review once.
3. **Bands** follow the analysis spec: < 0.1% ignore · 0.1–0.5% weak · 0.5–1% emerging · 1–3% meaningful · 3–5% very strong · > 5% high-priority.
4. **Mean ★** is the arithmetic mean of `rating` across the reviews in a theme.
5. **Lift** = (segment rate) ÷ (global rate).
6. **Text sub-cuts** are counted only inside a hand-coded theme and are always labelled as such; counts described as "read individually" come from re-reading every review in the theme.
7. **No review was excluded, down-weighted or de-duplicated.**

## D. Judgement calls and known error risk

1. **`PAY_BOUGHT`** includes trials, past purchases and reviewers who say they hold premium; it excludes stated intent ("I will buy").
2. **`PAY_PROMO` vs `PAY_CHARGE`:** `PAY_PROMO` is the advertised-discount-vs-charged-price mismatch; `PAY_CHARGE` is any other unexpected charge (trial conversion, accidental purchase on open, double charge). Reviews describing both carry both.
3. **`PAY_REGRESS` vs `PAY_RESTORE`:** `PAY_REGRESS` when a reviewer says something they had paid for now requires paying again; `PAY_RESTORE` when a purchase simply isn't recognised. `13845809502` and `13875929821` carry both.
4. **`STAT_CRASH` reviews also carry `BUG_CRASH`**, so `BUG_CRASH` (133) includes them; `STAT_CRASH` is the subset that names a statistics view.
5. **`BUG_LAUNCH` is a hang, not a crash** — the app stays on the splash screen. It is coded `BUG_CRASH` only when the reviewer also describes a crash (`12036500366`).
6. **Low information:** one-line praise without a named property ("great app", "很好") is `META_LOWINFO`; a named property ("simple", "reminders work") gets that property's code instead.
7. **`PAY_CAP3`** is applied when the three-habit limit is named or clearly implied ("only 3 free"), not for generic paywall complaints (`PAY_WALL`).
8. **`DES_CLEAN` vs `DES_PRETTY`:** ease and simplicity vs visual design and sounds; reviews praising both carry both.
9. **Contradictions** are coded only where the text and the rating clearly conflict; lukewarm 3★ praise is not coded.
10. **Ownership claims** (`9961528922`, `10350224024`, `12966824370`) are coded as stated (`USER_SIBLING`, `PAY_SCAM`, `PAY_REGRESS`) and are not verified.
11. **Storefront and language:** reviews are assigned to the storefront they were posted in, whatever their language.

**Known error risk overall:** the headline clusters — the offer mismatch (§0.2), statistics crashes (§0.3), support silence (§0.4), the cn launch stall (§0.5) and legacy re-subscription (§6.4) — were re-read in full and are described in plain words by the reviewers. The risk concentrates in the request codes (`CORE_EDIT`, `REQ_OTHER`, `STAT_WANT`), whose boundaries could move individual counts by ±10%, and in short reviews coded `DES_CLEAN` vs `META_LOWINFO`. **No recommendation in Part 9 rests on a theme smaller than 8 reviews**; F7 and R8 are included on data-integrity and trust grounds and say so.

## G. External sources used

| Source | What it supplied | Access | Date |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Tools/habit_apps_ranked.json` (this repository) | Public ratings (global 26,245 @ 4.6; per storefront), search ranks (best 2, median 26, 57 storefronts ranked), `market_tier` (high-spend definition, §7.2), audience label "moderate" | Local file; undated in-file | Read 13 Sep 2026 |
| `manifest.json`, `_state.json` (target folder) | Developer name, bundle ID, per-storefront counts, rating distribution, crawl completeness | Local files | Extracted 8 Sep 2026 |
| App Store listing / developer site | **Not used.** No network fetch was made; every feature, price and tier description is review-derived | — | — |

## H. Reproducibility

| Artefact | Path | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Read-through corpus | `Temp/53-all-reviews.txt`, `Temp/53-chunks/001–014.txt`, `Temp/53-index.tsv` | All 1,743 reviews in date order, 125 per chunk; index → review_id |
| Hand classification (index-keyed) | `Temp/53-cls/001–014.txt` | One line per review: `index CODE,CODE` — the judgement |
| Taxonomy | `Temp/53-themes.py` | 85 codes, 12 families, definitions |
| Resolver | `Temp/53-build-classification.py` | index → review_id; emits `Temp/53-review-classification.py` |
| Validator | `Temp/53-validate.py` | Zero unknown IDs / unassigned / duplicate keys / duplicate themes / undefined codes |
| Reconciliation | `Temp/53-reconcile.py` | Main file vs 76 country files vs manifest vs state |
| Aggregation | `Temp/53-common.py`, `Temp/53-aggregate.py`, `Temp/53-extra.py` | All counts, unions, segments, trends, sub-cuts |
| Quote lookup | `Temp/53-show.py` | Exact text for any ID or working index |
| Table generator | `Temp/53-tables.py` → `Temp/53-gen/` | Every generated table, ID list and the Part 10 index |
| Report build / verify | `Temp/53-report-src-01…06.md`, `Temp/53-build-report.py`, `Temp/53-verify-report.py` | Placeholder fill; post-write checks (cited IDs exist; Part 10 rows match; Appendix C lists match; §-references resolve) |

`Temp/` is gitignored; this report is the deliverable.

| Requirement | Status |
|---|---|
| Every record in the corpus read individually, in the original language | ✅ 1,743 / 1,743 — 14 sequential chunks, indices 1–1,743, no gaps |
| Every record included in global results | ✅ 1,743 classified, 0 unassigned, validated programmatically (§1.3) |
| No standalone claims for sub-50-review countries without a limited-evidence warning | ✅ Nine eligible storefronts analysed; all others marked [limited evidence] (§7.1) |
| Signal thresholds applied at global and eligible-country level | ✅ §3.1 (global), §7.3–§7.7 (per-country denominators) |
| High-spend and high-review-volume groups defined and compared | ✅ §7.2 |
| Paid-user evidence separated from general feedback | ✅ Part 6; direct vs indirect tiers; no conversion rate claimed |
| Purchase drivers and monetization friction both identified | ✅ §6.2 drivers, §6.3 barriers, §6.4 post-purchase, §6.5 refunds |
| Review IDs and numbers on every material claim | ✅ Throughout; complete ID lists in Appendix C |
| Solicited-review / burst patterns and rating contradictions disclosed | ✅ §1.7, §5.6, Appendix E |
| Time trends with a documented method | ✅ Part 8 |
| Complete per-review index | ✅ Part 10 — all 1,743 |
| Every cited review ID verified to exist in `reviews.jsonl` | ✅ `Temp/53-verify-report.py` (post-write) |
| One Markdown file | ✅ This file |
| Actionable, evidence-backed implications | ✅ Part 9 — F1–F7, M1–M5, R1–R8, Q1–Q5, each tied to findings |
